     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori update — refresh tool-owned scaffolded files after a CLI upgrade.
     4	 * Tool-owned: apriori/runbook.md (copied from the package's own RUNBOOK.md — single source)
     5	 * and per-tool command files that already exist (from templates/command.md).
     6	 * User-owned is NEVER touched: process-config.md, specs/, changes/, review/, truth/,
     7	 * and the rules files the init pointer was appended to (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, …).
     8	 * Zero deps — pure Node stdlib.
     9	 */
    10	const fs = require('fs');
    11	const path = require('path');
    12	const { TOOLS } = require('./init');
    13	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    14	const { legacyRoots } = require('./resolve');
    15	const managed = require('./managed');
    16	
    17	const PKG_ROOT = path.join(__dirname, '..');
    18	const RUNBOOK_SRC = path.join(PKG_ROOT, 'RUNBOOK.md');
    19	const COMMAND_SRC = path.join(PKG_ROOT, 'templates', 'command.md');
    20	
    21	// Refresh one existing target from src; returns 'updated' | 'up-to-date'.
    22	function refresh(target, src, dryRun) {
    23	  const want = fs.readFileSync(src, 'utf8');
    24	  if (fs.readFileSync(target, 'utf8') === want) return 'up-to-date';
    25	  if (!dryRun) fs.writeFileSync(target, want);
    26	  return 'updated';
    27	}
    28	
    29	const CURE = "delete it and rerun 'apriori init --tools <t>' to hand it back to the tool";
    30	
    31	// Refresh tool-owned files under root — but only those apriori/managed.json proves the
    32	// tool owns AND the user hasn't modified. Everything else is reported and left alone.
    33	// Pre-manifest projects are adopted on proof (runbook unconditionally; command files
    34	// only when their bytes match a shipped template generation).
    35	// Returns { actions: [{file, action}] }; throws on no runbook or manifest hygiene errors.
    36	function run(root, opts = {}) {
    37	  const rb = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'runbook.md');
    38	  if (!fs.existsSync(rb)) throw new Error(`no apriori/runbook.md here — run 'apriori init' first`);
    39	  const manifest = managed.readManifest(root, TOOLS);       // hygiene throws before any touch
    40	  const generations = opts.generations || managed.TEMPLATE_GENERATIONS;
    41	  const dry = !!opts.dryRun;
    42	  const actions = [];
    43	  const files = manifest ? { ...manifest.files } : {};
    44	  let manifestDirty = manifest === null;                    // adoption always materializes it
    45	
    46	  // one candidate: managed semantics when listed; adoption when pre-manifest; unmanaged otherwise
    47	  const consider = (rel, srcAbs, adoptable) => {
    48	    const p = path.join(root, rel);
    49	    const listed = manifest && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(manifest.files, rel);
    50	    if (!fs.existsSync(p)) {
    51	      if (listed) actions.push({ file: rel, action: "missing (skipped — recreating is init's job)" });
    52	      return;
    53	    }
    54	    managed.assertContained(root, rel);                     // before ANY read or hash
    55	    const cur = managed.hashFile(p);
    56	    if (listed) {
    57	      if (cur !== manifest.files[rel]) { actions.push({ file: rel, action: `modified (skipped — locally modified; ${CURE})` }); return; }
    58	      const act = refresh(p, srcAbs, dry);
    59	      actions.push({ file: rel, action: act });
    60	      if (act === 'updated') { files[rel] = managed.hashBytes(fs.readFileSync(srcAbs)); manifestDirty = true; }
    61	      return;
    62	    }
    63	    if (manifest === null && adoptable(cur)) {              // pre-manifest adoption on proof
    64	      const act = refresh(p, srcAbs, dry);
    65	      actions.push({ file: rel, action: act });
    66	      files[rel] = act === 'updated' ? managed.hashBytes(fs.readFileSync(srcAbs)) : cur;
    67	      return;
    68	    }
    69	    actions.push({ file: rel, action: `unmanaged (skipped — not created by this tool; ${CURE})` });
    70	  };
    71	
    72	  // runbook: adopted unconditionally pre-manifest (its refresh is update's reason to exist)
    73	  consider('apriori/runbook.md', opts.runbookSrc || RUNBOOK_SRC, () => true);
    74	  // protocol-required scaffolding the refreshed runbook relies on (IN-11): create if missing, NEVER modify
    75	  const gi = path.join(root, 'apriori', '.gitignore');
    76	  if (!fs.existsSync(gi)) {
    77	    if (!dry) { fs.writeFileSync(gi, 'tmp/\n'); fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'tmp'), { recursive: true }); }
    78	    actions.push({ file: 'apriori/.gitignore', action: 'created' });
    79	  }
    80	  for (const key of Object.keys(TOOLS)) {
    81	    const rel = TOOLS[key].command;
    82	    if (!rel) continue;
    83	    consider(rel, opts.commandSrc || COMMAND_SRC, (cur) => generations.includes(cur));
    84	  }
    85	  if (manifestDirty && !dry) managed.writeManifest(root, files);
    86	  // legacy 3.x layout roots: refreshing the protocol over pre-4.0 artifacts must be loud (UP-12)
    87	  const legacy = legacyRoots(root);
    88	  const warnings = legacy.length
    89	    ? [`legacy 3.x layout root(s) present: ${legacy.join(', ')} — migrate them into their change bundles, see MIGRATING.md (4.0)`]
    90	    : [];
    91	  return { actions, warnings };
    92	}
    93	
    94	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori update [--dry-run]';
    95	
    96	function cli(argv) {
    97	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'update', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0, flags: { '--dry-run': 'flag' } }, (f) => {
    98	    const dryRun = !!f['--dry-run'];
    99	    let res;
   100	    try { res = run(process.cwd(), { dryRun }); }
   101	    catch (e) { console.error('  ' + e.message); return 1; }
   102	    for (const { file, action } of res.actions)
   103	      console.log(`  ${action === 'updated' ? '✓' : '·'} ${file}  (${action})`);
   104	    for (const w of res.warnings || []) console.error(`  warning: ${w}`);
   105	    const n = res.actions.filter((a) => a.action === 'updated').length;
   106	    const v = require('../package.json').version;
   107	    console.log(n
   108	      ? `\n  ${n} file(s) ${dryRun ? 'would be ' : ''}refreshed to apriori-cli ${v}.`
   109	      : `\n  everything already matches apriori-cli ${v}.`);
   110	    console.log('  (user-owned files — process-config.md, specs/, changes/, rules files — are never touched.)');
   111	    return 0;
   112	  });
   113	}
   114	
   115	module.exports = { run, refresh, cli };

     1	'use strict';
     2	/*
     3	 * apriori doctor — diagnose the project↔apriori seam. Seven checks, one exit code:
     4	 * 0 = HEALTHY · 1 = FINDINGS · 2 = UNUSABLE (Node floor, uninitialized, usage error).
     5	 * Doctor never repairs and never writes; its ONLY side effect is D5 running the
     6	 * project's test command once (--no-run removes even that). Findings name their fixer.
     7	 */
     8	const fs = require('fs');
     9	const path = require('path');
    10	const { TOOLS, detectTools } = require('./init');
    11	const { checkRunbookFreshness } = require('./check');
    12	const { collectScenarios, parseTap, zeroTapParsed, runTestCommand, configTestCmd, DEFAULT_ID } = require('./spec-runner');
    13	const { activeChanges, parseFlowState } = require('./status');
    14	const { containsReal } = require('./archive-merge');
    15	const { withStrict } = require('./args');
    16	const { legacyRoots } = require('./resolve');
    17	
    18	const NODE_FLOOR = 22;
    19	
    20	// type-honest probes: doctor's whole job is malformed scaffolds — a file impersonating a
    21	// directory (or vice versa) must become a FINDING, never a false pass or a crash
    22	function isDir(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isDirectory(); } catch { return false; } }
    23	function isFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile(); } catch { return false; } }
    24	function readIfFile(p) { try { return fs.statSync(p).isFile() ? fs.readFileSync(p, 'utf8') : null; } catch { return null; } }
    25	
    26	// D5's classifier over a runTestCommand result — exported for the Windows-safe signal test (DR-06)
    27	function classifyProbe(run) {
    28	  // run = runTestCommand's shape: { out, status, signal, error }
    29	  const { out } = run;
    30	  if (run.error) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command failed to spawn: ${run.error}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    31	  if (run.signal) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command was killed by signal ${run.signal}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    32	  const idRe = new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID);
    33	  const { results, untagged, untaggedFails, bailout } = parseTap(out, idRe);
    34	  if (bailout) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test run aborted: ${bailout}`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    35	  if (!out.trim()) return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'test command produced no output', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    36	  if (zeroTapParsed(out, results, untagged))
    37	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'output is not TAP — node test runner needs --test-reporter=tap', fix: 'emit TAP from the test command' };
    38	  const parsed = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.pass + r.fail, 0) + untagged.length;
    39	  const failCount = [...results.values()].reduce((s, r) => s + r.fail, 0) + untaggedFails;
    40	  // an unexplained non-zero exit is classified BEFORE any ok branch — a non-zero `1..0` run lands here
    41	  if (run.status !== null && run.status !== 0 && failCount === 0)
    42	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: `test command exited ${run.status} unexplained by TAP — truncated?`, fix: 'fix the test command' };
    43	  if (parsed === 0) {
    44	    if (/^1\.\.0\b/m.test(out)) return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: 'TAP plumbing OK (empty suite, 1..0)' };
    45	    return { id: 'D5', status: 'finding', detail: 'TAP stream truncated or malformed (version/plan but zero result lines)', fix: 'fix the test command' };
    46	  }
    47	  const pass = parsed - failCount;
    48	  return { id: 'D5', status: 'ok', detail: `TAP plumbing OK (${pass}p/${failCount}f — test failures are verify's business, not doctor's)` };
    49	}
    50	
    51	// The whole diagnosis. → { code, result, findings, checks, errors }
    52	function runDoctor(opts = {}) {
    53	  const cwd = opts.cwd || process.cwd();
    54	  const checks = [], errors = [];
    55	  const finish = (code) => {
    56	    const findings = checks.filter((c) => c.status === 'finding').length;
    57	    const result = code === 2 ? 'UNUSABLE' : (findings ? 'FINDINGS' : 'HEALTHY');
    58	    return { code: code !== undefined ? code : (findings ? 1 : 0), result, findings, checks, errors };
    59	  };
    60	
    61	  // D1 — Node floor (version injectable for tests, DR-12)
    62	  const major = parseInt((opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node), 10);
    63	  if (major < NODE_FLOOR) {
    64	    checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'finding', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} is below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`, fix: 'upgrade Node' });
    65	    errors.push(`Node below the supported floor (>= ${NODE_FLOOR})`);
    66	    return finish(2);
    67	  }
    68	  checks.push({ id: 'D1', status: 'ok', detail: `Node ${opts.nodeVersion || process.versions.node} >= ${NODE_FLOOR}` });
    69	
    70	  // D2 — init scaffold
    71	  const ap = path.join(cwd, 'apriori');
    72	  if (!isDir(ap)) {
    73	    checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail: `no apriori/ here — not an apriori project yet`, fix: `run 'apriori init'` });
    74	    errors.push(`no apriori/ directory — run 'apriori init'`);
    75	    return finish(2);
    76	  }
    77	  const gaps = [];
    78	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md'))) gaps.push([`apriori/runbook.md ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')) ? 'is not a regular file' : 'missing'}`, `apriori init`]);
    79	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'specs'))) gaps.push([`apriori/specs/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'specs')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'missing (half-initialized scaffold)'}`, `apriori init`]);
    80	  const giText = readIfFile(path.join(ap, '.gitignore'));
    81	  if (giText === null || !/^tmp\/$/m.test(giText)) gaps.push(['apriori/.gitignore missing, unreadable, or missing its tmp/ line', `apriori update`]);
    82	  if (!isDir(path.join(ap, 'tmp'))) gaps.push([`apriori/tmp/ ${fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'tmp')) ? 'is not a directory' : 'dir missing'}`, `apriori update / mkdir`]);
    83	  if (gaps.length) for (const [detail, fix] of gaps) checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'finding', detail, fix });
    84	  else checks.push({ id: 'D2', status: 'ok', detail: 'scaffold complete' + (fs.existsSync(path.join(ap, 'process-config.md')) ? '' : ' (no process-config.md — optional, defaults apply)') });
    85	
    86	  // D3 — runbook freshness (absence OR wrong type is D2's finding; D3 must never contradict or crash)
    87	  if (!isFile(path.join(ap, 'runbook.md')))
    88	    checks.push({ id: 'D3', status: 'n/a', detail: 'runbook missing or not a regular file — see D2; freshness not checkable' });
    89	  else {
    90	    const warn = checkRunbookFreshness(cwd);
    91	    checks.push(warn.length
    92	      ? { id: 'D3', status: 'finding', detail: warn[0], fix: 'apriori update' }
    93	      : { id: 'D3', status: 'ok', detail: 'runbook copy matches the installed CLI' });
    94	  }
    95	
    96	  // D4 — tool pointers for DETECTED tools only
    97	  const detected = detectTools(cwd);
    98	  if (!detected.length) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no known AI-tool markers detected' });
    99	  else {
   100	    let broken = 0;
   101	    for (const key of detected) {
   102	      const t = TOOLS[key];
   103	      const rules = path.join(cwd, t.rules);
   104	      const rulesText = readIfFile(rules);
   105	      if (rulesText === null || !rulesText.includes('apriori/runbook.md')) {
   106	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: ${t.rules} ${rulesText !== null ? 'lost its runbook pointer' : (fs.existsSync(rules) ? 'is not a regular readable file' : 'is missing')}`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   107	        broken++;
   108	      }
   109	      if (t.command && !isFile(path.join(cwd, t.command))) {
   110	        checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'finding', detail: `${t.name}: command file ${t.command} is missing`, fix: 'apriori init' });
   111	        broken++;
   112	      }
   113	    }
   114	    if (!broken) checks.push({ id: 'D4', status: 'ok', detail: `pointers intact for: ${detected.join(', ')}` });
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  // D5 — TAP probe (the one declared side effect; --no-run skips)
   118	  const testCmd = opts.testCmd || configTestCmd(cwd);
   119	  if (!testCmd) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no test command configured — pass --test-cmd or set one via apriori init --test-cmd' });
   120	  else if (opts.noRun) checks.push({ id: 'D5', status: 'n/a', detail: 'probe skipped (--no-run)' });
   121	  else checks.push(classifyProbe(runTestCommand(testCmd, cwd)));
   122	
   123	  // D6 — store health (no test run; default ID pattern only)
   124	  const specsDir = path.join(ap, 'specs');
   125	  if (!fs.existsSync(specsDir)) checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no store dir — see D2' });
   126	  else {
   127	    const { byId, unidentified, files, duplicates } = collectScenarios([specsDir], new RegExp(DEFAULT_ID));
   128	    if (!files.length || (byId.size === 0 && unidentified.length === 0))
   129	      checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'n/a', detail: 'empty store — normal for a new project' });
   130	    else {
   131	      let bad = 0;
   132	      if (unidentified.length) { checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `scenario(s) without a bindable ${DEFAULT_ID} ID: ${unidentified.map(([, t]) => t.slice(0, 40)).join(' · ')}`, fix: 'add leading IDs' }); bad++; }
   133	      if (duplicates.length) { checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'finding', detail: `duplicate scenario IDs: ${duplicates.map((d) => d.id).join(', ')}`, fix: 'deduplicate IDs' }); bad++; }
   134	      if (!bad) checks.push({ id: 'D6', status: 'ok', detail: `${byId.size} scenario(s), all bindable, no duplicates` });
   135	    }
   136	  }
   137	
   138	  // D7 — changes overview
   139	  const actives = activeChanges(cwd);
   140	  const notes = [];
   141	  let d7bad = 0;
   142	  const changesRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes');
   143	  for (const c of actives) {
   144	    const fp = path.join(changesRoot, c, 'flow-state.md');
   145	    if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: no flow-state.md`, fix: 'apriori new / restore the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   146	    // per-file containment: a symlinked flow-state pointing outside the changes tree is
   147	    // diagnosed, never read (doctor must not leak outside content into its report)
   148	    if (!containsReal(changesRoot, fp)) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state.md escapes apriori/changes/ (symlink?) — not read`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   149	    let st;
   150	    try { st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8')); }
   151	    catch (e) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state unreadable (${e.message})`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; continue; }
   152	    if (!st.change) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state has no 'change:' key`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   153	    else if (st.change !== c) { checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'finding', detail: `${c}: flow-state says 'change: ${st.change}' — mismatches the dir`, fix: 'fix the file' }); d7bad++; }
   154	    else notes.push(`${c} @ ${st['current-step'] || '?'}`);
   155	  }
   156	  const archRoot = path.join(cwd, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   157	  if (fs.existsSync(archRoot)) {
   158	    for (const b of fs.readdirSync(archRoot).sort()) {
   159	      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{4}-/.test(b)) continue;
   160	      const dir = path.join(archRoot, b);
   161	      let isDir = false;
   162	      try { isDir = fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory(); } catch { continue; }
   163	      if (!isDir) continue;
   164	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, dir)) { notes.push(`${b}: skipped (escapes archive/ — symlink?)`); continue; }
   165	      const fp = path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md');
   166	      if (!fs.existsSync(fp)) continue;
   167	      if (!containsReal(archRoot, fp)) { notes.push(`${b}: flow-state escapes archive/ (symlink?) — not read`); continue; }
   168	      const st = parseFlowState(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8'));
   169	      const step = st['current-step'];
   170	      if (step && step !== 'DONE' && step !== 'ABANDONED') notes.push(`archived ${b} @ ${step} — gate ④ possibly pending`);
   171	    }
   172	  }
   173	  if (!d7bad) checks.push(actives.length || notes.length
   174	    ? { id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') || `${actives.length} active change(s), flow-states valid` }
   175	    : { id: 'D7', status: 'n/a', detail: 'no changes yet' });
   176	  else if (notes.length) checks.push({ id: 'D7', status: 'ok', detail: notes.join('; ') });
   177	
   178	  // D8 — legacy 3.x layout roots (existence-level, never followed or read through)
   179	  const legacy = legacyRoots(cwd);
   180	  checks.push(legacy.length
   181	    ? { id: 'D8', status: 'finding', detail: `legacy 3.x layout root(s) present: ${legacy.join(', ')}`, fix: 'migrate the artifacts into their change bundles — see MIGRATING.md (4.0)' }
   182	    : { id: 'D8', status: 'ok', detail: 'no legacy 3.x layout roots' });
   183	
   184	  return finish();
   185	}
   186	
   187	function toJson(res) {
   188	  return { result: res.result, findings: res.findings, checks: res.checks, errors: res.errors };
   189	}
   190	
   191	const USAGE = 'usage: apriori doctor [--test-cmd "<cmd>"] [--no-run] [--cwd <dir>] [--json]';
   192	
   193	function cli(argv) {
   194	  return withStrict(argv, { sub: 'doctor', usage: USAGE, positionals: 0,
   195	    flags: { '--test-cmd': 'value', '--no-run': 'flag', '--cwd': 'value', '--json': 'flag' },
   196	    jsonError: (m) => JSON.stringify({ result: 'UNUSABLE', findings: 0, checks: [], errors: [m] }, null, 2) }, (f) => {
   197	    const a = { testCmd: f['--test-cmd'] || null, noRun: !!f['--no-run'], cwd: f['--cwd'] || process.cwd(), json: !!f['--json'] };
   198	    const res = runDoctor(a);
   199	    if (a.json) { console.log(JSON.stringify(toJson(res), null, 2)); return res.code; }
   200	    for (const e of res.errors) console.error('doctor: ' + e);
   201	    const mark = { ok: '✓', finding: '✗', 'n/a': '–' };
   202	    for (const c of res.checks) console.log(`${mark[c.status]} ${c.id} ${c.detail}${c.fix ? `  → ${c.fix}` : ''}`);
   203	    console.log(res.code === 2 ? '\nDOCTOR: UNUSABLE (see errors)'
   204	      : res.findings ? `\nDOCTOR: ${res.findings} finding(s)` : '\nDOCTOR: HEALTHY');
   205	    return res.code;
   206	  });
   207	}
   208	
   209	module.exports = { runDoctor, classifyProbe, cli };

    11	function run(args, cwd) { return spawnSync('node', [BIN, ...args], { encoding: 'utf8', cwd }); }
    12	function tapCmd(...lines) {
    13	  return `node -e "${lines.map((l) => `console.log('${l}')`).join(';')}"`;
    14	}

    16	function mkProject(files) {
    17	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-gate-'));
    18	  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
    19	    const p = path.join(root, rel);
    20	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
    21	    fs.writeFileSync(p, content);
    22	  }
    23	  return root;
    24	}

    26	const STORE = '### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 base\n- t\n';

    28	const FLOW = (name, tier = 'medium') => `change: ${name}\ntier: ${tier}\ntrack: harden\ntrack-rationale: r\nlineage: v3\ncurrent-step: STEP5\nround: 1\nnext-action: x\ngates:\n  - 2026-07-11T00:00 note: n\n`;

   101	test('GT-05 verdict evidence is mechanical (missing raw blocks; raw fixes; symlink blocks)', () => {
   102	  const root = healthy();
   103	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1.md'), 'body\nVERDICT: no major issues\n');
   104	  let r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   105	  let c5 = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5');
   106	  assert.strictEqual(c5.status, 'blocked');
   107	  assert.match(c5.detail, /req-review-v1/);
   108	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt'), 'raw transcript');
   109	  r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   110	  c5 = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5');
   111	  assert.strictEqual(c5.status, 'pass', c5.detail);
   112	  // design-dir docs participate too
   113	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/spec-review-v1.md'), 'VERDICT: 1 issues open\n');
   114	  r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   115	  assert.strictEqual(r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5').status, 'blocked');
   116	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/spec-review-v1-raw.txt'), 'raw');
   117	  r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   118	  assert.strictEqual(r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5').status, 'pass');
   119	  // a VERDICT-free doc needs no raw
   120	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/kb-check.md'), 'notes only\n');
   121	  assert.strictEqual(gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5').status, 'pass');
   122	  // symlinked doc-glob match blocks (where the platform allows symlinks)
   123	  let canSymlink = true;
   124	  try { fs.symlinkSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1.md'), path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/c-linked.md')); }
   125	  catch { canSymlink = false; }
   126	  if (canSymlink) {
   127	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   128	    c5 = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5');
   129	    assert.strictEqual(c5.status, 'blocked');
   130	    assert.match(c5.detail, /c-linked/);
   131	    fs.rmSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/c-linked.md'));
   132	    // a SYMLINKED raw is not evidence: replace the real raw with a symlink → blocked again
   133	    fs.renameSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt'), path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/elsewhere.txt'));
   134	    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/elsewhere.txt'), path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt'));
   135	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   136	    assert.strictEqual(r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C5').status, 'blocked');
   137	    // a DANGLING review/ symlink is a defect, not absence — C4 and C5 both block, in-flight…
   138	    const dang = healthy();
   139	    fs.rmSync(path.join(dang, 'apriori/changes/c/review'), { recursive: true });
   140	    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(dang, 'no-such-target'), path.join(dang, 'apriori/changes/c/review'));
   141	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: dang, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   142	    for (const id of ['C4', 'C5']) {
   143	      const c = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === id);
   144	      assert.strictEqual(c.status, 'blocked', `${id}: ${c.detail}`);
   145	      assert.match(c.detail, /symlink/);
   146	    }
   147	    // …and archived
   148	    const darch = mkProject({
   149	      'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE + '\n### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 new\n- t\n',
   150	      'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/flow-state.md': FLOW('c'),
   151	      'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/tasks.md': '- [x] T1\n',
   152	      'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/specs/kv/spec.md': DELTA,
   153	    });
   154	    fs.symlinkSync(path.join(darch, 'no-such-target'), path.join(darch, 'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/review'));
   155	    r = gate.runGate({ cwd: darch, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   156	    for (const id of ['C4', 'C5']) {
   157	      const c = r.checks.find((x) => x.id === id);
   158	      assert.strictEqual(c.status, 'blocked', `${id}: ${c.detail}`);
   159	      assert.match(c.detail, /symlink/);
   160	    }
   161	  }
   162	});

   164	test('GT-06 the binding gate is stage-aware (in-flight projected; archived plain)', () => {
   165	  // in-flight: XB-01 exists only in the delta — passing C1 proves the projection ran
   166	  const inflight = healthy();
   167	  const r1 = gate.runGate({ cwd: inflight, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   168	  assert.strictEqual(r1.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C1').status, 'pass');
   169	  // gaps → blocked with counts
   170	  const r1b = gate.runGate({ cwd: inflight, change: 'c', testCmd: tapCmd('ok 1 - XA-01 a') });
   171	  const c1b = r1b.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C1');
   172	  assert.strictEqual(c1b.status, 'blocked');
   173	  assert.match(c1b.detail, /unbound/);
   174	  // archived: change only under archive/, store already merged, plain verify runs
   175	  const arch = mkProject({
   176	    'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE + '\n### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 new\n- t\n',
   177	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/flow-state.md': FLOW('c'),
   178	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/tasks.md': '- [x] T1\n',
   179	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/specs/kv/spec.md': DELTA,
   180	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/review/issues.md': LEDGER_OK,
   181	  });
   182	  const r2 = gate.runGate({ cwd: arch, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   183	  assert.strictEqual(r2.stage, 'archived');
   184	  assert.strictEqual(r2.code, 0, JSON.stringify(r2.checks));
   185	});

   187	test('GT-07 resolution is validated and deterministic', () => {
   188	  const root = healthy();
   189	  // invalid name → exit 2 (spawned: also proves usage surface)
   190	  const bad = run(['gate', '--change', '../evil', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK], root);
   191	  assert.strictEqual(bad.status, 2);
   192	  // not found → exit 2 naming both locations
   193	  const nf = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'nope', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   194	  assert.strictEqual(nf.code, 2);
   195	  assert.ok(nf.errors.some((e) => e.includes('apriori/changes') || e.includes('archive')), nf.errors.join());
   196	  // two archived dirs → lexicographically last basename wins
   197	  const arch = mkProject({
   198	    'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE + '\n### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 new\n- t\n',
   199	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/flow-state.md': FLOW('c'),
   200	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/tasks.md': '- [ ] stale unchecked\n',
   201	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/specs/kv/spec.md': DELTA,
   202	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1400-c/flow-state.md': FLOW('c'),
   203	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1400-c/tasks.md': '- [x] fresh checked\n',
   204	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1400-c/specs/kv/spec.md': DELTA,
   205	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1400-c/review/issues.md': LEDGER_OK,
   206	  });
   207	  const r = gate.runGate({ cwd: arch, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   208	  assert.strictEqual(r.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C2').status, 'pass');   // newer dir used
   209	  // a stray FILE with a stamp-shaped name is ignored, older real dir still wins deterministically
   210	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(arch, 'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1600-c'), 'not a dir');
   211	  const r2 = gate.runGate({ cwd: arch, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   212	  assert.strictEqual(r2.stage, 'archived');
   213	  assert.strictEqual(r2.checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C2').status, 'pass'); // still the 1400 dir, not the file
   214	  // an archived entry symlinking OUTSIDE archive/ (but inside changes/) is an escape → exit 2
   215	  let canSymlink = true;
   216	  const elsewhere = path.join(arch, 'apriori/changes/elsewhere-c');
   217	  fs.mkdirSync(elsewhere, { recursive: true });
   218	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(elsewhere, 'flow-state.md'), FLOW('c'));
   219	  try { fs.symlinkSync(elsewhere, path.join(arch, 'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1700-c')); }
   220	  catch { canSymlink = false; }
   221	  if (canSymlink) {
   222	    const r3 = gate.runGate({ cwd: arch, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   223	    assert.strictEqual(r3.code, 2);
   224	    assert.ok(r3.errors.some((e) => e.includes('escape')), r3.errors.join());
   225	  }
   226	});

   261	test('GT-10 KB freshness degrades honestly', () => {
   262	  // n/a branches need no git: no truth doc at all
   263	  const root = healthy();
   264	  const na = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C6');
   265	  assert.strictEqual(na.status, 'n/a');
   266	  // truth doc without lib file → n/a
   267	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/truth'), { recursive: true });
   268	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/truth/kv.md'), 'source-commit: deadbeef\n');
   269	  const na2 = gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C6');
   270	  assert.strictEqual(na2.status, 'n/a');
   271	  // full git fixture — guarded on git availability
   272	  const g = spawnSync('git', ['--version'], { encoding: 'utf8' });
   273	  if (!g.error && g.status === 0) {
   274	    const root3 = healthy();
   275	    const env = { ...process.env, GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: 't', GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: 't@t', GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: 't', GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: 't@t' };
   276	    const git = (...a) => { const r = spawnSync('git', ['-C', root3, ...a], { encoding: 'utf8', env }); assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0, r.stderr); return r; };
   277	    git('init', '-q');
   278	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root3, 'lib'), { recursive: true });
   279	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root3, 'lib/kv.js'), '1');
   280	    git('add', '-A'); git('commit', '-q', '-m', 'one');
   281	    const c1 = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD').stdout.trim();
   282	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/truth'), { recursive: true });
   283	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/truth/kv.md'), `source-commit: ${c1}\n`);
   284	    let c6 = gate.runGate({ cwd: root3, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C6');
   285	    assert.strictEqual(c6.status, 'pass', c6.detail);           // stamp up to date
   286	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root3, 'lib/kv.js'), '2');
   287	    git('add', '-A'); git('commit', '-q', '-m', 'two');
   288	    c6 = gate.runGate({ cwd: root3, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C6');
   289	    assert.strictEqual(c6.status, 'blocked');                   // stale stamp
   290	    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/truth/kv.md'), 'source-commit: 0000000\n');
   291	    c6 = gate.runGate({ cwd: root3, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C6');
   292	    assert.strictEqual(c6.status, 'n/a');                       // bad commit = infra, never a block
   293	  }
   294	});

   296	test('GT-11 --json is pure JSON in every outcome class', () => {
   297	  const root = healthy();
   298	  const cases = [
   299	    [['gate', '--change', 'c', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK, '--json'], 0, 'PASS', 'in-flight'],
   300	    [['gate', '--change', 'nope', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK, '--json'], 2, 'ERROR', null],
   301	    [['gate', '--change', '../evil', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK, '--json'], 2, 'ERROR', null],
   302	    [['gate', '--json'], 2, 'ERROR', null],                                   // usage: --change missing
   303	  ];
   304	  for (const [args, code, result, stage] of cases) {
   305	    const r = run(args, root);
   306	    assert.strictEqual(r.status, code, args.join(' ') + ': ' + r.stdout + r.stderr);
   307	    const j = JSON.parse(r.stdout);
   308	    assert.strictEqual(j.result, result);
   309	    assert.strictEqual(j.stage, stage);
   310	    assert.ok(Array.isArray(j.checks) && Array.isArray(j.errors));
   311	  }
   312	  assert.strictEqual(JSON.parse(run(['gate', '--json'], root).stdout).change, null);
   313	  // BLOCKED class
   314	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/tasks.md'), '- [ ] nope\n');
   315	  const b = run(['gate', '--change', 'c', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK, '--json'], root);
   316	  assert.strictEqual(b.status, 1);
   317	  assert.strictEqual(JSON.parse(b.stdout).result, 'BLOCKED');
   318	  // missing flow-state class: still pure JSON, stage resolved, result ERROR
   319	  fs.rmSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'));
   320	  const nofs = run(['gate', '--change', 'c', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK, '--json'], root);
   321	  assert.strictEqual(nofs.status, 2);
   322	  const jn = JSON.parse(nofs.stdout);
   323	  assert.strictEqual(jn.result, 'ERROR');
   324	  assert.ok(jn.errors.length > 0);
   325	  // verify-untrustworthy class (broken delta → projection fails): pure JSON, ERROR
   326	  const bad = healthy('d');
   327	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(bad, 'apriori/changes/d/specs/kv/spec.md'), '# prose, zero ops\n');
   328	  const vu = run(['gate', '--change', 'd', '--test-cmd', TAP_OK, '--json'], bad);
   329	  assert.strictEqual(vu.status, 2);
   330	  assert.strictEqual(JSON.parse(vu.stdout).result, 'ERROR');
   331	});

   333	test('GT-12 gate is read-only', () => {
   334	  const root = healthy();
   335	  const snap = () => {
   336	    const out = [];
   337	    const walk = (d) => { for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) { const p = path.join(d, e.name); if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p); else out.push(p + ':' + fs.statSync(p).size + ':' + fs.statSync(p).mtimeMs); } };
   338	    walk(root);
   339	    return out.sort().join('\n');
   340	  };
   341	  const before = snap();
   342	  gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   343	  gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'nope', testCmd: TAP_OK });
   344	  assert.strictEqual(snap(), before);
   345	});
   346	
   347	// ---- ledger-states (GT-13..15): C4 speaks the terminal-state vocabulary ----
   348	
   349	const LEDGER_HDR = '| ID | Issue | Risk | Round found | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n';
   350	const ledgerWith = (...rows) => LEDGER_HDR + rows.map((r) => `| ${r[0]} | i | low | 1 | ${r[1]} |\n`).join('');
   351	// FLOW plus an extra gates: entry line (the human waive record)
   352	const FLOW_G = (name, extra) => FLOW(name) + (extra ? `  - ${extra}\n` : '');
   353	
   354	function archProject(ledger, flowExtra) {
   355	  return mkProject({
   356	    'apriori/specs/kv/spec.md': STORE + '\n### Requirement: Beta\n\n#### Scenario: XB-01 new\n- t\n',
   357	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/flow-state.md': FLOW_G('c', flowExtra),
   358	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/tasks.md': '- [x] T1\n',
   359	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/specs/kv/spec.md': DELTA,
   360	    'apriori/changes/archive/2026-07-10T1200-c/review/issues.md': ledger,
   361	  });
   362	}
   363	const c4Of = (root) => gate.runGate({ cwd: root, change: 'c', testCmd: TAP_OK }).checks.find((x) => x.id === 'C4');
   364	
   365	test('GT-13 archived ledgers must be terminal', () => {
   366	  // fixed at archived stage → blocked with the reviewer-verify cure
   367	  const f = c4Of(archProject(ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'fixed (v2)'])));
   368	  assert.strictEqual(f.status, 'blocked');
   369	  assert.match(f.detail, /reviewer must verify|never verified/);
   370	  // plain reasoned rejected → blocked with the concurrence cure
   371	  const r = c4Of(archProject(ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'rejected — cosmetic, out of scope'])));
   372	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 'blocked');
   373	  assert.match(r.detail, /rejected-verified|reviewer concurrence/);
   374	  // unknown status → blocked naming the vocabulary
   375	  const u = c4Of(archProject(ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'done'])));
   376	  assert.strictEqual(u.status, 'blocked');
   377	  assert.match(u.detail, /vocabulary/);
   378	  // all-terminal → pass (waived backed by a gates: entry naming the ID)
   379	  const ok = c4Of(archProject(
   380	    ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'verified'],
   381	               ['Q-2', 'rejected-verified — cosmetic; reviewer concurred (review-v2)'],
   382	               ['Q-3', 'waived — owner accepts the perf risk'],
   383	               ['Q-4', 'advisory-acked']),
   384	    '2026-07-12T01:00 gate: owner waived Q-3 (perf risk accepted for this release)'));
   385	  assert.strictEqual(ok.status, 'pass', ok.detail);
   386	});
   387	
   388	test('GT-14 waives belong to humans, unknown states belong to nobody', () => {
   389	  // in-flight: waived without any gates: evidence → blocked
   390	  const root1 = healthy();
   391	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root1, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   392	  const w1 = c4Of(root1);
   393	  assert.strictEqual(w1.status, 'blocked');
   394	  assert.match(w1.detail, /gates: entry/);
   395	  // same row passes once the human decision is recorded in gates:
   396	  const root2 = healthy();
   397	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root2, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   398	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root2, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'),
   399	    '  - 2026-07-12T01:00 owner waived Q-1: risk accepted\n');
   400	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root2).status, 'pass');
   401	  // exact ID token: an entry waiving Q-10 never satisfies row Q-1
   402	  const root3 = healthy();
   403	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   404	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root3, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'),
   405	    '  - 2026-07-12T01:00 owner waived Q-10: a different row\n');
   406	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root3).status, 'blocked');
   407	  // ID in one entry + 'waived' in another entry never passes (same-entry rule)
   408	  const root4 = healthy();
   409	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root4, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'waived — accepted']));
   410	  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root4, 'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md'),
   411	    '  - 2026-07-12T01:00 note: Q-1 discussed\n  - 2026-07-12T01:01 something else waived here\n');
   412	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root4).status, 'blocked');
   413	  // unknown status blocks IN-FLIGHT too; reasonless terminals block
   414	  const root5 = healthy();
   415	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root5, 'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md'), ledgerWith(['Q-1', 'verifed']));
   416	  assert.strictEqual(c4Of(root5).status, 'blocked');
   417	  const root6 = healthy();

    13	function mkProject(files) {
    14	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-arch-'));
    15	  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
    16	    const p = path.join(root, rel);
    17	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), { recursive: true });
    18	    fs.writeFileSync(p, content);
    19	  }
    20	  return root;
    21	}

    67	  const root = twoModuleProject();

    69	  const ops = {
    70	    writeFileSync: fs.writeFileSync.bind(fs),
    71	    rmSync: fs.rmSync.bind(fs),
    72	    renameSync: (a, b) => { renames++; if (renames === 2) throw new Error('injected rename failure'); fs.renameSync(a, b); },
    73	  };

   203	    fs.symlinkSync(outsideMod, path.join(proj3, 'apriori/changes/c/specs/a'));
   204	    const r3 = run(['archive', '--change', 'c'], proj3);
   205	    assert.strictEqual(r3.status, 2);
   206	    // a symlinked move DESTINATION (archive/) pointing outside is rejected before any move
   207	    const proj4 = twoModuleProject();
   208	    const outsideArch = path.join(root, 'outside', 'arch');
   209	    fs.mkdirSync(outsideArch, { recursive: true });
   210	    fs.symlinkSync(outsideArch, path.join(proj4, 'apriori/changes/archive'));
   211	    const r4 = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write', '--changes-dir', 'apriori/changes'], proj4);
   212	    assert.strictEqual(r4.status, 2);
   213	    assert.match(r4.stderr, /archive destination/);
   214	    assert.doesNotMatch(fs.readFileSync(path.join(proj4, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8'), /Alpha2/);  // nothing written
   215	  }
   216	});
   217	
   218	test('AM-23 malformed or duplicated stamps are hygiene errors', () => {
   219	  const good = am.fingerprint(STORE_A);
   220	  const cases = [
   221	    `<!-- apriori-base: sha256:zzz -->\n${ADD_A}`,                                        // malformed digest
   222	    `<!-- apriori-base: ${good} -->\n<!-- apriori-base: new -->\n${ADD_A}`,               // two stamps
   223	    `${ADD_A}\n<!-- apriori-base: ${good} -->\n`,                                         // stamp after first section
   224	    `<!-- apriori-base ${good} -->\n${ADD_A}`,                                            // structurally malformed (no colon) — must NOT silently disable CAS
   225	  ];
   226	  for (const bad of cases) {
   227	    const root = twoModuleProject({ 'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': bad });
   228	    const r = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], root);
   229	    assert.strictEqual(r.status, 1, bad.slice(0, 30));
   230	    assert.match(r.stdout + r.stderr, /a[\/\\]spec\.md/);
   231	  }
   232	});

   234	test('AM-24 a diverged stamp stops archive before any write, on both surfaces', () => {
   235	  const stale = `<!-- apriori-base: sha256:${'0'.repeat(64)} -->\n${ADD_A}`;
   236	  // high-level form
   237	  const root1 = twoModuleProject({ 'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': stale });

   243	  // single-file form
   244	  const root2 = mkProject({ 'store.md': STORE_A, 'delta.md': stale });
   245	  const r2 = run(['archive', '--store', 'store.md', '--delta', 'delta.md', '--change', 'c', '--write'], root2);
   246	  assert.strictEqual(r2.status, 1);
   247	  assert.match(r2.stdout + r2.stderr, /sha256:/);
   248	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root2, 'store.md'), 'utf8'), STORE_A);
   249	});

   260	test('AM-26 the new sentinel matches only an absent store', () => {

   263	  const root = mkProject({

   276	  assert.strictEqual(r2.status, 1);

   280	test('AM-27 apriori stamp prints the current stamp line (all CLI branches)', async (t) => {
   281	  const root = mkProject({ 'store.md': 'alpha\r\nbeta\n' });
   282	  await t.test('existing file → matching stamp line, exit 0', () => {
   283	    const r = run(['stamp', 'store.md'], root);
   284	    assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0);
   285	    assert.strictEqual(r.stdout.trim(), `<!-- apriori-base: ${am.fingerprint('alpha\r\nbeta\n')} -->`);
   286	  });
   287	  await t.test('CRLF normalization: same fingerprint as LF content', () => {
   288	    assert.strictEqual(am.fingerprint('alpha\r\nbeta\n'), am.fingerprint('alpha\nbeta\n'));
   289	    assert.strictEqual(am.fingerprint('alpha\rbeta\n'), am.fingerprint('alpha\nbeta\n'));
   290	  });
   291	  await t.test('absent path → the new form, exit 0', () => {
   292	    const r = run(['stamp', 'no-such.md'], root);
   293	    assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0);
   294	    assert.strictEqual(r.stdout.trim(), '<!-- apriori-base: new -->');
   295	  });
   296	  await t.test('directory → error naming the path, exit 2', () => {
   297	    const r = run(['stamp', '.'], root);
   298	    assert.strictEqual(r.status, 2);
   299	  });
   300	  await t.test('zero or multiple arguments → usage, exit 2', () => {
   301	    assert.strictEqual(run(['stamp'], root).status, 2);
   302	    assert.strictEqual(run(['stamp', 'a.md', 'b.md'], root).status, 2);
   303	  });
   304	});

   306	test('AM-10 REMOVED rerun: already-deprecated by this change is a no-op; by another change conflicts', () => {
   307	  const removed = { ADDED: new Map(), MODIFIED: new Map(), REMOVED: new Map([['Alpha', '### Requirement: Alpha\n']]), RENAMED: [] };
   308	  // first application deprecates
   309	  const first = am.merge(STORE_A, removed, 'c');
   310	  assert.deepStrictEqual(first.deprecated, ['Alpha']);
   311	  const afterText = am.renderStore(STORE_A, first.store);
   312	  // rerun on the deprecated store (same change) → no-op, not a conflict
   313	  const rerun = am.merge(afterText, removed, 'c');
   314	  assert.strictEqual(rerun.conflicts.length, 0);
   315	  assert.ok(rerun.unchanged.some((u) => u.includes('Alpha')));
   316	  // deprecated by a DIFFERENT change → conflict
   317	  const other = am.merge(afterText, removed, 'other-change');
   318	  assert.ok(other.conflicts.length > 0);
   319	});

   321	// ---- delta-consumption (AM-28..31): the parser consumes its whole input ----
   322	
   323	// reference: the pre-rewrite split()-based parse, kept verbatim so the corpus
   324	// test can assert the walker reproduces it byte-identically on well-formed input
   325	function referenceParseDelta(text) {
   326	  const SECTION = /^##\s+(ADDED|MODIFIED|REMOVED|RENAMED)\s+Requirements\s*$/m;
   327	  const REQ = /^###\s+Requirement:\s+(.+?)\s*$([\s\S]*?)(?=^###\s+Requirement:|$(?![\s\S]))/gm;
   328	  const buckets = { ADDED: new Map(), MODIFIED: new Map(), REMOVED: new Map(), RENAMED: [] };
   329	  const parts = text.split(SECTION);
   330	  for (let i = 1; i < parts.length; i += 2) {
   331	    const kind = parts[i];
   332	    if (kind === 'RENAMED') {
   333	      for (const m of parts[i + 1].matchAll(/^\s*-\s*(.+?)\s*->\s*(.+?)\s*$/gm))
   334	        buckets.RENAMED.push([m[1].trim(), m[2].trim()]);
   335	    } else {
   336	      let m; REQ.lastIndex = 0;
   337	      while ((m = REQ.exec(parts[i + 1])) !== null) {
   338	        const name = m[1].trim();
   339	        if (!buckets[kind].has(name)) buckets[kind].set(name, m[0].replace(/\s+$/, '') + '\n');
   340	      }
   341	    }
   342	  }
   343	  return buckets;
   344	}
   345	
   346	test('AM-28 a misspelled section heading is reported, never absorbed', () => {
   347	  const delta = '# Delta — x (c)\n\n## ADDED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Fine\n\n#### Scenario: DC-01 a\n- t\n\n## ADDDED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: WronglyClassified\n\n#### Scenario: DC-02 b\n- t\n';
   348	  const { delta: buckets, problems } = am.parseDeltaStrict(delta);
   349	  assert.ok(problems.some((p) => p.includes('ADDDED') && /line 10\b/.test(p)), JSON.stringify(problems));
   350	  for (const kind of ['ADDED', 'MODIFIED', 'REMOVED'])
   351	    assert.ok(!buckets[kind].has('WronglyClassified'), `WronglyClassified leaked into ${kind}`);
   352	  assert.ok(buckets.ADDED.has('Fine'));
   353	  // both surfaces fail closed
   354	  const root = twoModuleProject({ 'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': delta });
   355	  assert.strictEqual(run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], root).status, 1);
   356	  const r = run(['verify', '--change', 'c'], mkProject({
   357	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE_A,
   358	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': delta,
   359	  }));
   360	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 2);
   361	});
   362	
   363	test('AM-29 structure outside its legal home is reported with line numbers', () => {
   364	  const delta = '### Requirement: Early\n\n## RENAMED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: InsideRenamed\n- Old -> New\n\n## ADDED Requirements\n\n#### Scenario: DC-03 stray\n\n### Requirement: Ok\n\n#### Scenario: DC-04 fine\n- t\n';
   365	  const { delta: buckets, problems } = am.parseDeltaStrict(delta);
   366	  assert.ok(problems.some((p) => /line 1\b/.test(p) && /before any section/i.test(p)), JSON.stringify(problems));
   367	  assert.ok(problems.some((p) => /line 5\b/.test(p) && /RENAMED/.test(p)), JSON.stringify(problems));
   368	  assert.ok(problems.some((p) => /line 10\b/.test(p) && /[Ss]cenario/.test(p)), JSON.stringify(problems));
   369	  assert.ok(buckets.ADDED.has('Ok'), 'legal block still parsed');
   370	  assert.ok(!buckets.ADDED.has('Early') && !buckets.ADDED.has('InsideRenamed'));
   371	  assert.deepStrictEqual(buckets.RENAMED, [], 'the illegal block body is never reinterpreted as rename lines');
   372	});
   373	
   374	test('AM-30 free text and fences stay legal; CRLF parses identically', () => {
   375	  const delta = '# Delta — archive-merge (c)\n\nA note before any section.\n\n## ADDED Requirements\n\nSection preamble prose is fine.\n\n### Requirement: Fenced\n\nBody prose.\n\n```md\n## Bogus Heading\n### Requirement: InsideFence\n<!-- apriori-base: sha256:zzz -->\n```\n\n#### Scenario: DC-05 f\n- t\n\nTrailing notes without structure markers.\n';
   376	  const lf = am.parseDeltaStrict(delta);
   377	  assert.deepStrictEqual(lf.problems, []);
   378	  assert.ok(lf.delta.ADDED.has('Fenced'));
   379	  assert.ok(!lf.delta.ADDED.has('InsideFence'));
   380	  assert.match(lf.delta.ADDED.get('Fenced'), /Bogus Heading/, 'fenced lines stay in the body');
   381	  const crlf = am.parseDeltaStrict(delta.replace(/\n/g, '\r\n'));
   382	  assert.deepStrictEqual(crlf.problems, []);
   383	  assert.deepStrictEqual([...crlf.delta.ADDED.entries()], [...lf.delta.ADDED.entries()], 'CRLF block text identical to LF');
   384	});

   386	test('AM-30b regression corpus: every archived delta parses clean and identical to the old grammar', () => {
   387	  const archRoot = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive');
   388	  if (!fs.existsSync(archRoot)) return;   // corpus is local-only (apriori/* untracked)
   389	  const files = [];
   390	  (function walk(d) {
   391	    for (const e of fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true })) {
   392	      const p = path.join(d, e.name);
   393	      if (e.isDirectory()) walk(p);
   394	      else if (e.isFile() && p.includes(`${path.sep}specs${path.sep}`) && p.endsWith('.md')) files.push(p);
   395	    }
   396	  })(archRoot);
   397	  for (const f of files) {
   398	    const text = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8');
   399	    const r = am.parseDeltaStrict(text);
   400	    assert.deepStrictEqual(r.problems, [], `${f}: ${JSON.stringify(r.problems)}`);
   401	    const ref = referenceParseDelta(text);
   402	    for (const kind of ['ADDED', 'MODIFIED', 'REMOVED']) {
   403	      assert.deepStrictEqual([...r.delta[kind].entries()], [...ref[kind].entries()], `${f} ${kind} differs from old grammar`);
   404	    }
   405	    assert.deepStrictEqual(r.delta.RENAMED, ref.RENAMED, `${f} RENAMED differs from old grammar`);
   406	  }
   407	});
   408	
   409	test('AM-31 stamp problems carry line numbers', () => {
   410	  const good = am.fingerprint(STORE_A);
   411	  const cases = [
   412	    [`<!-- apriori-base sha256:${'0'.repeat(64)} -->\n${ADD_A}`, /line 1\b/],   // malformed attempt (no colon)
   413	    [`<!-- apriori-base: ${good} -->\n<!-- apriori-base: new -->\n${ADD_A}`, /line 2\b/],  // duplicate
   414	    [`<!-- apriori-base: sha256:zzz -->\n${ADD_A}`, /line 1\b/],                // malformed digest
   415	    [`${ADD_A}\n<!-- apriori-base: ${good} -->\n`, /line 8\b/],                 // late stamp (ADD_A is 7 lines)
   416	  ];
   417	  for (const [text, lineRe] of cases) {
   418	    const { problems } = am.parseDeltaStrict(text);
   419	    assert.ok(problems.length > 0, text.slice(0, 40));
   420	    assert.ok(problems.some((p) => lineRe.test(p)), `${text.slice(0, 40)} → ${JSON.stringify(problems)}`);
   421	  }
   422	  // a stamp under a skipped unrecognized heading is attributed to NOTHING — never the delta stamp
   423	  const skipped = am.parseDeltaStrict(`## ADDDED Requirements\n\n<!-- apriori-base: new -->\n\n${ADD_A}`);
   424	  assert.strictEqual(skipped.stamp, null);
   425	  assert.ok(skipped.problems.some((p) => p.includes('ADDDED')));
   426	  assert.ok(!skipped.problems.some((p) => /apriori-base/.test(p)), 'covered by the heading problem, no flood');
   427	});

   429	// ---- cas-enforcement (AM-32..35): graded stamps + rerun repair ----
   430	
   431	const MOD_SAME = '## MODIFIED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 a\n- t\n';   // trim-equal to STORE_A's block
   432	const MOD_DIFF = '## MODIFIED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 a\n- CHANGED\n';
   433	const STALE = `<!-- apriori-base: sha256:${'0'.repeat(64)} -->\n`;
   434	
   435	test('AM-32 unstamped mutation deltas are denied on both archive forms', () => {
   436	  // high-level form: errors, writes nothing
   437	  const root = twoModuleProject({ 'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': MOD_DIFF });
   438	  const r = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], root);
   439	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 1, r.stdout + r.stderr);
   440	  assert.match(r.stdout + r.stderr, /unstamped mutation delta/);
   441	  assert.match(r.stdout + r.stderr, /apriori stamp/);
   442	  assert.doesNotMatch(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8'), /CHANGED/);
   443	  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c')), 'change dir not moved');
   444	  // single-file form denies too
   445	  const root2 = mkProject({ 'store.md': STORE_A, 'delta.md': MOD_DIFF });
   446	  const r2 = run(['archive', '--store', 'store.md', '--delta', 'delta.md', '--change', 'c', '--write'], root2);
   447	  assert.strictEqual(r2.status, 1);
   448	  assert.match(r2.stdout + r2.stderr, /unstamped mutation delta/);
   449	  assert.doesNotMatch(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root2, 'store.md'), 'utf8'), /CHANGED/);
   450	  // ADDED-only stays exempt and quiet
   451	  const root3 = twoModuleProject();
   452	  const r3 = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write'], root3);
   453	  assert.strictEqual(r3.status, 0);
   454	  assert.doesNotMatch(r3.stdout + r3.stderr, /unstamped mutation delta/);
   455	});
   456	
   457	test('AM-33 an already-applied stamped delta reruns to completion', () => {
   458	  // store already carries the merged content; the stamp records a stale base → mismatch + all-unchanged
   459	  const root = mkProject({
   460	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE_A,
   461	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': STALE + MOD_SAME,     // MODIFIED-only, trim-equal (the CE-1 case)
   462	  });
   463	  const before = fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8');
   464	  const r = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write', '--changes-dir', 'apriori/changes'], root);
   465	  assert.strictEqual(r.status, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
   466	  assert.match(r.stdout + r.stderr, /rerun accepted/);
   467	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8'), before);
   468	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c')), 'change dir must have moved');
   469	  assert.ok(fs.readdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/archive')).some((b) => b.endsWith('-c')));
   470	});
   471	
   472	test('AM-34 divergence with pending work never repairs; the resumed mix completes', () => {
   473	  // (a) stale stamp + a REAL pending op → hard fail, nothing written or moved
   474	  const rootA = mkProject({
   475	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE_A,
   476	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': STALE + MOD_DIFF,
   477	  });
   478	  const beforeA = fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootA, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8');
   479	  const rA = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write', '--changes-dir', 'apriori/changes'], rootA);
   480	  assert.strictEqual(rA.status, 1);
   481	  assert.match(rA.stdout + rA.stderr, /base mismatch/);
   482	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootA, 'apriori/specs/a/spec.md'), 'utf8'), beforeA);
   483	  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(rootA, 'apriori/changes/c')), 'change dir must NOT move');
   484	  // (b) the resumed-partial-commit mix: A stale-but-applied + B matching-with-real-ops → completes
   485	  const goodB = am.fingerprint(STORE_B);
   486	  const rootB = mkProject({
   487	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE_A,
   488	    'apriori/specs/b/spec.md': STORE_B,
   489	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': STALE + MOD_SAME,
   490	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/b/spec.md': `<!-- apriori-base: ${goodB} -->\n` + ADD_B,
   491	  });
   492	  const rB = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write', '--changes-dir', 'apriori/changes'], rootB);
   493	  assert.strictEqual(rB.status, 0, rB.stdout + rB.stderr);
   494	  assert.match(rB.stdout + rB.stderr, /rerun accepted/);
   495	  assert.match(fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootB, 'apriori/specs/b/spec.md'), 'utf8'), /Bravo2/);
   496	  // (c) mixed with a diverged pending file → whole preflight fails, B untouched too
   497	  const rootC = mkProject({
   498	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE_A,
   499	    'apriori/specs/b/spec.md': STORE_B,
   500	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': STALE + MOD_SAME,
   501	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/b/spec.md': STALE + '## MODIFIED Requirements\n\n### Requirement: Bravo\n\n#### Scenario: XV-01 b\n- CHANGED\n',
   502	  });
   503	  const rC = run(['archive', '--change', 'c', '--write', '--changes-dir', 'apriori/changes'], rootC);
   504	  assert.strictEqual(rC.status, 1);
   505	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(rootC, 'apriori/specs/b/spec.md'), 'utf8'), STORE_B);
   506	});
   507	
   508	test('AM-35 MODIFIED speaks the idempotence vocabulary', () => {
   509	  const sameBlock = '### Requirement: Alpha\n\n#### Scenario: XA-01 a\n- t\n';
   510	  const same = am.merge(STORE_A, { ADDED: new Map(), MODIFIED: new Map([['Alpha', sameBlock]]), REMOVED: new Map(), RENAMED: [] }, 'c');
   511	  assert.deepStrictEqual(same.modified, []);
   512	  assert.ok(same.unchanged.includes('Alpha'), JSON.stringify(same.unchanged));
   513	  const diff = am.merge(STORE_A, { ADDED: new Map(), MODIFIED: new Map([['Alpha', sameBlock.replace('- t', '- x')]]), REMOVED: new Map(), RENAMED: [] }, 'c');
   514	  assert.deepStrictEqual(diff.modified, ['Alpha']);
   515	});

   518	// ---- change-bundle (AM-36..39): the atomic move carries the whole bundle ----
   519	
   520	function bundleProject(extra = {}) {
   521	  return mkProject({
   522	    'apriori/specs/a/spec.md': STORE_A,
   523	    'apriori/changes/c/flow-state.md': 'change: c\ntier: medium\n',
   524	    'apriori/changes/c/specs/a/spec.md': ADD_A,
   525	    'apriori/changes/c/requirement/req-v1.md': 'v1',
   526	    'apriori/changes/c/requirement/req-final.md': 'final',
   527	    'apriori/changes/c/requirement/intent-card.md': 'card',
   528	    'apriori/changes/c/review/issues.md': '| ID | Issue | Risk | Round found | Status |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Q-1 | i | low | 1 | verified |\n',
   529	    'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1.md': 'VERDICT: no major issues\n',
   530	    'apriori/changes/c/review/req-review-v1-raw.txt': 'raw',
   531	    'apriori/changes/c/gap-report.md': 'gap',
   532	    ...extra,
   533	  });
   534	}
   535	const bundleArchived = (root) => {
   536	  const dirs = fs.readdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/archive')).filter((b) => b.endsWith('-c'));
   537	  return path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/archive', dirs[0]);
   538	};

   548	  assert.strictEqual(fs.readFileSync(path.join(A, 'gap-report.md'), 'utf8'), 'gap');
   549	  assert.ok(!fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'apriori/changes/c')), 'nothing left behind');
   550	});

     1	'use strict';
     2	const { test } = require('node:test');
     3	const assert = require('node:assert');
     4	const fs = require('node:fs');
     5	const os = require('node:os');
     6	const path = require('node:path');
     7	const status = require('../lib/status');
     8	
     9	function project(changes) {
    10	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-st-'));
    11	  for (const [name, { flow, ledger }] of Object.entries(changes)) {
    12	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name), { recursive: true });
    13	    if (flow) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name, 'flow-state.md'), flow);
    14	    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'review'), { recursive: true });
    15	    if (ledger) { fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name, 'review'), { recursive: true }); fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', name, 'review', 'issues.md'), ledger); }
    16	  }
    17	  return root;
    18	}
    19	
    20	const FLOW = `change: demo
    21	tier: medium
    22	track: harden
    23	current-step: STEP2
    24	next-action: spawn P5 reviewer   # 2026-07-07
    25	gates:
    26	  - 2026-07-07T02:00 gate③: approved
    27	`;
    28	const LEDGER = `| ID | Issue | Risk | Round | Status |
    29	|---|---|---|---|---|
    30	| D-1 | x | med | 1 | open |
    31	| D-2 | y | low | 1 | fixed |
    32	| D-3 | z | med | 2 | open |
    33	| D-4 | w | low | 2 | advisory-acked |
    34	`;

    36	test('ST-01 --change reports step, next-action, and open-ledger count/IDs', () => {
    37	  const root = project({ demo: { flow: FLOW, ledger: LEDGER } });
    38	  const s = status.changeStatus(root, 'demo');
    39	  assert.strictEqual(s.state['current-step'], 'STEP2');
    40	  assert.match(s.state['next-action'], /spawn P5 reviewer/);
    41	  assert.deepStrictEqual(s.open.map((r) => r.id), ['D-1', 'D-3']);
    42	  const out = status.formatOne(s);
    43	  assert.match(out, /step:.*STEP2/);
    44	  assert.match(out, /next-action:.*spawn P5 reviewer/);
    45	  assert.match(out, /last gate:.*gate③: approved/);          // last gate surfaced
    46	  assert.match(out, /open ledger:  2 — D-1, D-3/);
    47	});

    49	test('ST-02 no args lists active changes (with step + open count), excluding archive/', () => {

    51	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive', '2026-07-01-old'), { recursive: true });
    52	  assert.deepStrictEqual(status.activeChanges(root), ['demo', 'other']);   // archive/ excluded
    53	  // the no-args CLI output lists each change with its step and open count
    54	  const cwd = process.cwd(), log = console.log, out = [];
    55	  console.log = (...a) => out.push(a.join(' '));
    56	  try { process.chdir(root); assert.strictEqual(status.cli([]), 0); }
    57	  finally { console.log = log; process.chdir(cwd); }
    58	  const printed = out.join('\n');
    59	  assert.match(printed, /demo  —  STEP2, 2 open/);
    60	  assert.match(printed, /other  —  STEP0, 0 open/);
    61	  assert.doesNotMatch(printed, /2026-07-01-old/);            // archive not listed

    71	test('ST-04 --json emits a machine-consumable report (single + list), pure JSON', () => {
    72	  const root = project({ demo: { flow: FLOW, ledger: LEDGER } });
    73	  const cwd = process.cwd(), log = console.log, out = [];
    74	  console.log = (...a) => out.push(a.join(' '));
    75	  try {
    76	    process.chdir(root);
    77	    assert.strictEqual(status.cli(['--change', 'demo', '--json']), 0);
    78	    const single = JSON.parse(out.join('\n'));            // parses = pure JSON, no prose
    79	    assert.strictEqual(single.change, 'demo');
    80	    assert.strictEqual(single.step, 'STEP2');
    81	    assert.strictEqual(single.tier, 'medium');

    86	    assert.deepStrictEqual(single.openLedger, ['D-1', 'D-3']);
    87	    out.length = 0;
    88	    assert.strictEqual(status.cli(['--json']), 0);         // list mode
    89	    const list = JSON.parse(out.join('\n'));
    90	    assert.strictEqual(list.changes.length, 1);
    91	    assert.strictEqual(list.changes[0].change, 'demo');
    92	  } finally { console.log = log; process.chdir(cwd); }
    93	});

   100	function archivedProject() {
   101	  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'apriori-st-'));
   102	  const dir = path.join(root, 'apriori', 'changes', 'archive', '2026-07-10T1200-demo');
   103	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'review'), { recursive: true });
   104	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'flow-state.md'), FLOW.replace('STEP2', 'DONE'));
   105	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'review', 'issues.md'), LEDGER.replace(/open/g, 'verified'));
   106	  return root;
   107	}

   140	    fs.rmSync(path.join(root2, 'apriori/changes/demo/flow-state.md'));

**LEDGER DELTA**

| ID | Issue | Risk | Suggested requirement delta | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| RT-1 | `archive` 内符号链接条目“一律结构错误”范围不清。若解释为任意归档 symlink 都让所有 `resolveChange` 失败，会把无关手工残留变成全局熔断；若只检查匹配当前 change 的候选，又和“一律”冲突。 | P1 | 明确 resolver 只对当前查询名的 active candidate、匹配 `<stamp>-<name>` 的 archived candidate、以及 trust roots fail-closed；无关 archive 条目由 doctor/check 诊断或忽略。匹配候选 symlink 判结构错误是合理的。 | open |
| RT-2 | stamp “语义校验”只写月 01-12、日 01-31，会接受 `2026-02-31T2359-x`、非闰年 `02-29`。这仍是伪时间戳参与排序。 | P1 | 要求 Gregorian round-trip 校验：年月日时分解析后重新构造并逐字段相等；闰日合法，2/31、4/31 非法。非法匹配当前 name 的 stamp 目录应 exit 2，且不得被较旧合法目录掩盖。 | open |
| RT-3 | `validateChangeName` 的单一来源未约束错误分类顺序，可能改变 `new` 的既有用户可见文案：date-prefix、reserved、shape 三类现在有不同消息。 | P2 | 规定 validator 返回机器 kind：`invalid-shape` / `date-prefixed` / `reserved`，各 CLI 保留现有文案；date-prefix 检查覆盖 `YYYY-MM-*`、`YYYY-MM-DDT*` 等 date-looking 输入。保留名目前只需 `archive`，但 archive 单文件/高层表面也必须拒绝。 | open |
| RT-4 | `fileReadDefect` 祖先 walk 的结果契约仍是字符串式，`status` 现有逻辑依赖 `missing:` 前缀；共享实现时容易把 optional ledger 和 unsafe ancestor 混淆。 | P1 | 明确返回结构化 kind：`null | {kind:'missing'|'symlink'|'not-directory'|'not-file'|'escape'|'unreadable', path, ancestor?}`；leaf ENOENT 时只在 bundle root 内向上 walk，遇到 symlink/非目录祖先为 defect，纯缺失链才是 `missing`。 | open |
| RT-5 | D riders 把 MIGRATING、package、doctor/update 消息和 living store drift 混在一句里，范围不够清楚。`MIGRATING.md` 是 plain doc/package edit；gate/archive-merge/doctor/protocol specs 是 store-delta MODIFIED。 | P2 | 拆成两类：plain edits 为 `package.json files += MIGRATING.md`、D8/update 本地路径+稳定 URL、`MIGRATING.md` 表文案；store deltas 为 `gate/spec.md` waiver 句、`archive-merge/spec.md` AM-25、`doctor/spec.md` D1-D8/Node >=22，并视需要 MODIFIED `protocol/spec.md` 来绑定 package-files/URL/MIGRATING 检查。 | open |
| RT-6 | “本仓库 + 两沙盒 + ai-company 均应照常 PASS”不可机械验证：需求未给 repo 路径、命令、环境变量或无法访问时的降级规则。 | P2 | 写成可执行验收矩阵：本 repo 跑 `npm test` 与 `npm run verify`；外部 corpus 用明确路径或 env vars（如 `APRIORI_REGRESSION_ROOTS`）逐个跑 `apriori gate/status/verify` 的指定命令；路径不存在则记录 manual skip，不得算自动 PASS。 | open |

| RT-4 | `fileReadDefect` 祖先 walk 的结果契约仍是字符串式，`status` 现有逻辑依赖 `missing:` 前缀；共享实现时容易把 optional ledger 和 unsafe ancestor 混淆。 | P1 | 明确返回结构化 kind：`null | {kind:'missing'|'symlink'|'not-directory'|'not-file'|'escape'|'unreadable', path, ancestor?}`；leaf ENOENT 时只在 bundle root 内向上 walk，遇到 symlink/非目录祖先为 defect，纯缺失链才是 `missing`。 | open |
| RT-5 | D riders 把 MIGRATING、package、doctor/update 消息和 living store drift 混在一句里，范围不够清楚。`MIGRATING.md` 是 plain doc/package edit；gate/archive-merge/doctor/protocol specs 是 store-delta MODIFIED。 | P2 | 拆成两类：plain edits 为 `package.json files += MIGRATING.md`、D8/update 本地路径+稳定 URL、`MIGRATING.md` 表文案；store deltas 为 `gate/spec.md` waiver 句、`archive-merge/spec.md` AM-25、`doctor/spec.md` D1-D8/Node >=22，并视需要 MODIFIED `protocol/spec.md` 来绑定 package-files/URL/MIGRATING 检查。 | open |
| RT-6 | “本仓库 + 两沙盒 + ai-company 均应照常 PASS”不可机械验证：需求未给 repo 路径、命令、环境变量或无法访问时的降级规则。 | P2 | 写成可执行验收矩阵：本 repo 跑 `npm test` 与 `npm run verify`；外部 corpus 用明确路径或 env vars（如 `APRIORI_REGRESSION_ROOTS`）逐个跑 `apriori gate/status/verify` 的指定命令；路径不存在则记录 manual skip，不得算自动 PASS。 | open |
